### קובץ ה-HTML המלא להרצה (`torah_shaar_styles.html`)

שמור את הקוד שלהלן כקובץ HTML ופתח אותו ישירות בדפדפן:

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="he" dir="rtl">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>גלריית 11 סטי שערים ועיטורים תורניים לדפי A4</title>
<style>
  @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Bellefair&family=David+Libre:wght@400;700&family=Frank+Ruhl+Libre:wght@400;700;900&family=Noto+Serif+Hebrew:wght@400;700;900&family=Assistant:wght@400;600;700&display=swap');

  :root {
    --fn-sh-title: 'Bellefair', 'Frank Ruhl Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-title: 44pt;
    --fn-sh-sub: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-sub: 20pt;
    --fn-sh-desc: 'Frank Ruhl Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-desc: 11.5pt;
    --fn-sh-sec: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-sec: 11pt;
    --fn-sh-author: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-author: 14pt;
    --fn-sh-year: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-year: 11.5pt;
  }

  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

  body {
    background-color: #1e293b;
    color: #0f172a;
    font-family: 'David Libre', 'Frank Ruhl Libre', serif;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    height: 100vh;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  /* Top Navigation Bar */
  #top-nav {
    background: #0f172a;
    color: #f8fafc;
    padding: 10px 20px;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    border-bottom: 2px solid #b8860b;
    box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
    z-index: 1000;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .nav-title {
    font-size: 14pt;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #facc15;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
  }
  .nav-controls {
    display: flex;
    gap: 8px;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
  .set-picker {
    background: #1e293b;
    color: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #b8860b;
    padding: 6px 12px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    font-family: 'David Libre', serif;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
  .btn-action {
    background: #b8860b;
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    padding: 6px 12px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    font-weight: 700;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 9.5pt;
    transition: 0.2s;
  }
  .btn-action:hover {
    background: #d4af37;
  }

  /* Live Metadata Bar */
  #metadata-bar {
    background: #f1f5f9;
    border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
    padding: 8px 16px;
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 12px;
    align-items: center;
    font-size: 9pt;
    font-family: 'Assistant', sans-serif;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .meta-field {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 5px;
  }
  .meta-field label {
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #475569;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .meta-field input {
    padding: 3px 6px;
    border: 1px solid #94a3b8;
    border-radius: 3px;
    font-size: 9pt;
    font-family: 'David Libre', serif;
  }

  /* Main Workspace */
  #main-workspace {
    display: flex;
    flex: 1;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  /* Sidebar */
  #sets-sidebar {
    width: 320px;
    background: #ffffff;
    border-left: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
    overflow-y: auto;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    padding: 12px;
    box-shadow: -2px 0 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .sidebar-section-title {
    font-size: 11pt;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #1e293b;
    border-bottom: 2px solid #b8860b;
    padding-bottom: 4px;
    margin-bottom: 10px;
  }
  .set-card {
    border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 10px;
    margin-bottom: 8px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    background: #f8fafc;
  }
  .set-card:hover {
    border-color: #b8860b;
    background: #fffbeb;
  }
  .set-card.active {
    border-color: #b8860b;
    background: #fef08a;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(184, 134, 11, 0.4);
  }
  .set-card-title {
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    color: #0f172a;
  }
  .set-card-desc {
    font-size: 8.5pt;
    color: #64748b;
    margin-top: 3px;
    line-height: 1.3;
  }

  /* Preview Workspace */
  #preview-pane {
    flex: 1;
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: 25px 20px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 20px;
    background-color: #334155;
  }

  /* View Mode Tabs */
  .preview-tabs {
    display: flex;
    gap: 6px;
    margin-bottom: -10px;
    z-index: 10;
  }
  .preview-tab {
    padding: 7px 16px;
    background: #1e293b;
    color: #cbd5e1;
    border-radius: 6px 6px 0 0;
    font-weight: 700;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 10pt;
    border: 1px solid #475569;
    border-bottom: none;
    transition: 0.15s;
  }
  .preview-tab:hover {
    color: #fff;
  }
  .preview-tab.active {
    background: #ffffff;
    color: #0f172a;
    border-color: #ffffff;
  }

  /* A4 Page Representation */
  .a4-page {
    width: 210mm;
    height: 297mm;
    min-height: 297mm;
    max-height: 297mm;
    background: #ffffff;
    box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    padding: 0;
    margin-bottom: 25px;
  }

  /* Main Shaar Content Framework */
  .shaar-main-content {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    position: relative;
    z-index: 20;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 22mm 16mm 18mm 16mm;
  }
  .bsd-header { font-size: 11pt; font-weight: 700; color: #333; }
  .sefer-label { font-size: 16pt; letter-spacing: 5px; font-weight: 700; margin-top: 4px; }
  .book-title-main { font-family: var(--fn-sh-title); font-size: var(--sz-sh-title); line-height: 1.08; margin: 8px 0 4px 0; font-weight: 900; }
  .book-subtitle { font-family: var(--fn-sh-sub); font-size: var(--sz-sh-sub); font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.25; }
  .book-description { font-family: var(--fn-sh-desc); font-size: var(--sz-sh-desc); line-height: 1.45; max-width: 68%; margin: 8px auto; }
  .book-sections-summary { font-family: var(--fn-sh-sec); font-size: var(--sz-sh-sec); line-height: 1.4; border-top: 1px solid #bbb; border-bottom: 1px solid #bbb; padding: 6px 12px; margin: 8px 0; font-weight: 700; width: 70%; }
  .author-block { font-family: var(--fn-sh-author); font-size: calc(var(--sz-sh-author) * 0.88); line-height: 1.3; }
  .author-name { font-size: var(--sz-sh-author); font-weight: 700; }
  .publication-footer { font-family: var(--fn-sh-year); font-size: var(--sz-sh-year); font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 1px; }

  /* Sub Shaar Specific Layout Framework */
  .sub-shaar-page {
    position: relative;
    padding: 0;
  }
  .sub-shaar-content-area {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0; bottom: 0; left: 0; width: 55%;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 25mm 14mm;
    z-index: 20;
  }

  /* Component Cards */
  .component-preview-card {
    background: #ffffff;
    width: 210mm;
    padding: 20px 25px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    margin-bottom: 15px;
  }
  .card-label {
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #1e293b;
    margin-bottom: 12px;
    border-bottom: 1px dashed #cbd5e1;
    padding-bottom: 5px;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
  }

  .svg-bg-full {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%;
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 5;
  }

  @media print {
    body { background: none; overflow: visible; }
    #top-nav, #metadata-bar, #sets-sidebar, .preview-tabs { display: none !important; }
    #preview-pane { padding: 0; background: none; }
    .a4-page { box-shadow: none; margin: 0; page-break-after: always; break-after: page; }
  }
</style>
</head>
<body>

  <!-- Top Bar -->
  <div id="top-nav">
    <div class="nav-title">
      <span>👑</span>
      <span>11 סטי שערים ועיטורים תורניים מהודרים (A4)</span>
    </div>
    <div class="nav-controls">
      <select id="theme-select" class="set-picker" onchange="handleSelectChange(this.value)">
        <!-- Populated dynamically -->
      </select>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('single')">📄 סט נבחר</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('all_main')">📚 כל 11 השערים הראשיים</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('all_sub')">📑 כל שערי המשנה</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('all_elem')">✨ כל העיטורים</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ הדפסה / PDF</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Live Metadata Editor -->
  <div id="metadata-bar">
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>שם הספר:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-title" value="ספר מלכי המלוכה" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>כותרת משנה:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-subtitle" value="חידושים וביאורים עמוקים בסוגיות הש&quot;ס" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>נושא מפורט:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-desc" value="בירור שיטות הראשונים והאחרונים ובירורי הלכות למעשה בעיון נמרץ ובסברא ישרה" oninput="renderCurrentView()" style="width:250px;">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>פרטי מהדורא:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-sec" value="מהדורא שניה ומורחבת בסייעתא דשמיא" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>שם המחבר:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-author" value="ידידיה בר אבא הלוי" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>מקום ושנה:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-year" value="ירושלים עיה&quot;ק תובב&quot;א — שנת ה'תשפ&quot;ו לפ&quot;ק" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
  </div>

  <div id="main-workspace">
    <!-- Sidebar -->
    <div id="sets-sidebar">
      <div class="sidebar-section-title">11 ערכות הנושא (Sets)</div>
      <div id="sets-list-container">
        <!-- Populated dynamically -->
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Preview Pane -->
    <div id="preview-pane">
      <div class="preview-tabs" id="preview-tabs-bar">
        <div class="preview-tab active" id="tab-all" onclick="handleTabClick('all')">כל 4 הרכיבים בסט זה</div>
        <div class="preview-tab" id="tab-main" onclick="handleTabClick('main')">שער ראשי (A4)</div>
        <div class="preview-tab" id="tab-sub" onclick="handleTabClick('sub')">שער משנה (A4)</div>
        <div class="preview-tab" id="tab-elem" onclick="handleTabClick('elem')">עיטורי סיום וכותרת</div>
      </div>

      <div id="display-container" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; width:100%;">
        <!-- Rendered output -->
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    /* Database of all 11 Authentic Torah Vector Sets */
    const TORAH_SETS = [
      {
        id: 1,
        name: "עמודי ספירלה וגמלון שבור",
        origin: "בהשראת תמונה 0 (אגרתא דחדוותא)",
        desc: "עמודים קורינתיים עטופים בסרט ספירלי מתלפף, גמלון שבור עליון עם קשת וסלסולי אקנתוס.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<defs>' +
              '<linearGradient id="grad-p-1" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="0">' +
                '<stop offset="0%" stop-color="#cbd5e1"/><stop offset="35%" stop-color="#ffffff"/><stop offset="70%" stop-color="#e2e8f0"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#94a3b8"/>' +
              '</linearGradient>' +
              '<linearGradient id="grad-r-1" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">' +
                '<stop offset="0%" stop-color="#475569"/><stop offset="50%" stop-color="#0f172a"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#334155"/>' +
              '</linearGradient>' +
            '</defs>' +
            '<rect x="25" y="25" width="744" height="1073" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
            '<rect x="30" y="30" width="734" height="1063" fill="none" stroke="#777" stroke-width="0.75" stroke-dasharray="4,2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 45 160 L 290 35 L 365 110 C 365 110 335 135 335 160 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 749 160 L 504 35 L 429 110 C 429 110 459 135 459 160 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 335 160 C 335 90, 459 90, 459 160" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2.5"/>' +
            '<circle cx="397" cy="115" r="16" fill="none" stroke="#333" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +

            '<g id="spiral-pillar-r">' +
              '<rect x="45" y="970" width="85" height="60" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +

        },
        renderSubSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="25" y="25" width="744" height="1073" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
            '<rect x="436" y="25" width="333" height="1073" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="none"/>' +
            '<path d="M 25 150 L 250 45 L 340 120 L 436 120 L 436 150 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +

            '<path d="M 400 300 Q 432 335 465 310 L 465 345 Q 432 370 400 335 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111"/>' +
            '<path d="M 400 500 Q 432 535 465 510 L 465 545 Q 432 570 400 535 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111"/>' +
            '<path d="M 400 700 Q 432 735 465 710 L 465 745 Q 432 770 400 735 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111"/>' +

        dividerSVG: '<svg style="width:220px; height:26px; margin:auto;" viewBox="0 0 400 40"><g stroke="#222" fill="none" stroke-width="2"><path d="M10,20 L160,20"/><path d="M240,20 L390,20"/><circle cx="200" cy="20" r="12" fill="#fff" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2.5"/><circle cx="200" cy="20" r="5" fill="#222"/><path d="M175,10 Q200,20 175,30" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M225,10 Q200,20 225,30" stroke-width="2"/></g></svg>',
        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:11px; width:48px;" viewBox="0 0 100 24"><path d="M5,12 L40,12 M60,12 L95,12" stroke="#444" stroke-width="2"/><polygon points="50,4 58,12 50,20 42,12" fill="#444"/></svg>'

      {

        desc: "שולי תחרה וילנאית עשירים ורחבים בשני צדי הדף עם תבנית סלסולים צפופה ומסגרת מרכזית עדינה.",

            '<rect x="145" y="45" width="504" height="1033" fill="none" stroke="#1e293b" stroke-width="1.2"/>' +

        },
        renderSubSVG: function() {

        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:12px; width:50px;" viewBox="0 0 100 24"><path d="M5,12 C25,4 35,20 50,12 C65,4 75,20 95,12" fill="none" stroke="#444" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="50" cy="12" r="3" fill="#444"/></svg>'

      {

        desc: "קשתות כפולות מעוטרות בסלסולי קצוות וכרכובים גותיים, שושנות בפינות ומסגרת רבת-שכבות.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="30" y="30" width="734" height="1063" rx="8" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="3"/>' +
            '<rect x="40" y="40" width="714" height="1043" rx="6" fill="none" stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1"/>' +

              '<path d="M 45 1078 Q 80 1078 85 1038 Q 45 1043 45 1078 Z"/>' +
              '<path d="M 749 1078 Q 714 1078 709 1038 Q 749 1043 749 1078 Z"/>' +
            '</g>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        renderSubSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="30" y="30" width="734" height="1063" rx="8" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2.5"/>' +

            '<path d="M 70 290 C 70 180, 240 150, 410 150 L 410 935 C 240 935, 70 905, 70 790 Z" fill="none" stroke="#64748b" stroke-dasharray="5,3"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        dividerSVG: '<svg style="width:200px; height:24px; margin:auto;" viewBox="0 0 260 30"><path d="M10,15 C60,5 90,25 130,15 C170,5 200,25 250,15" fill="none" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="130" cy="15" r="5" fill="#222"/><circle cx="70" cy="15" r="3" fill="#666"/><circle cx="190" cy="15" r="3" fill="#666"/></svg>',
        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:12px; width:44px;" viewBox="0 0 100 24"><path d="M10,12 Q30,2 50,12 Q70,22 90,12" fill="none" stroke="#333" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="50" cy="12" r="3.5" fill="#333"/></svg>'
      },

      {
        id: 4,
        name: "כתר מלכות ועלי אקנתוס",
        origin: "בהשראת תמונה 3 (ספר שלחן מלכים)",
        desc: "כתר תורה מלכותי בראש הדף, מסגרת עלי אקנתוס עשירה בסגנון דפוסי איטליה הקדומים ומדליון חותם בבסיס.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="35" y="35" width="724" height="1053" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="45" y="45" width="704" height="1033" fill="none" stroke="#64748b" stroke-width="0.8"/>' +
            '<g transform="translate(337, 50)">' +
              '<rect x="10" y="55" width="100" height="12" rx="3" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
              '<path d="M 10 55 L 20 20 L 40 42 L 60 10 L 80 42 L 100 20 L 110 55 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
              '<circle cx="20" cy="18" r="4" fill="#111"/>' +
              '<circle cx="60" cy="8" r="5" fill="#111"/>' +
              '<circle cx="100" cy="18" r="4" fill="#111"/>' +
            '</g>' +

              '<line x1="0" y1="200" x2="85" y2="200" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="300" x2="85" y2="300" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="400" x2="85" y2="400" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="500" x2="85" y2="500" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="600" x2="85" y2="600" stroke="#333"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<path d="M 40 260 C 40 120, 350 120, 350 260" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        dividerSVG: '<svg style="width:200px; height:24px; margin:auto;" viewBox="0 0 240 30"><g fill="#222"><polygon points="120,4 128,15 120,26 112,15"/><polygon points="90,7 96,15 90,23 84,15"/><polygon points="150,7 156,15 150,23 144,15"/><line x1="10" y1="15" x2="75" y2="15" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/><line x1="165" y1="15" x2="230" y2="15" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/></g></svg>',
        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:12px; width:45px;" viewBox="0 0 90 24"><polygon points="45,4 52,12 45,20 38,12" fill="#222"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="30" y2="12" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/><line x1="60" y1="12" x2="85" y2="12" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/></svg>'
      },

      {
        id: 11,
        name: "אכסדרת ארמון בפרספקטיבה עם עומק ורצפת שיש",
        origin: "בהשראת תמונה 10 (שולחן ערוך המבואר - מתיבתא)",
        desc: "שער פרספקטיבה תלת-מימדי מהודר עם רצפת שיש מדורגת, עמודי שיש כפולים, וילונות קשורים וקשת מקומרת.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<g stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1" fill="#f8fafc">' +
              '<polygon points="200,820 594,820 744,980 50,980"/>' +
              '<line x1="397" y1="820" x2="397" y2="980"/>' +
              '<line x1="330" y1="820" x2="220" y2="980"/><line x1="464" y1="820" x2="574" y2="980"/>' +
              '<line x1="265" y1="820" x2="70" y2="980"/><line x1="529" y1="820" x2="724" y2="980"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<path d="M 200 320 C 200 150, 594 150, 594 320 L 594 820 L 200 820 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 230 340 C 230 190, 564 190, 564 340 L 564 820 L 230 820 Z" fill="none" stroke="#64748b" stroke-width="1.2"/>' +
            '<rect x="40" y="40" width="714" height="80" fill="#f1f5f9" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2.5"/>' +
            '<rect x="70" y="160" width="85" height="700" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="639" y="160" width="85" height="700" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="40" y="980" width="714" height="70" fill="#e2e8f0" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2.5"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        renderSubSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="40" y="40" width="714" height="1043" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="436" y="40" width="318" height="1043" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="none"/>' +
            '<g stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1" fill="#f8fafc">' +
              '<polygon points="120,820 436,820 436,980 50,980"/>' +
              '<line x1="280" y1="820" x2="280" y2="980"/><line x1="200" y1="820" x2="140" y2="980"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<rect x="375" y="160" width="60" height="700" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 50 320 C 50 160, 375 160, 375 320 L 375 820 L 50 820 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.8"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        dividerSVG: '<svg style="width:230px; height:26px; margin:auto;" viewBox="0 0 300 35"><g stroke="#222" fill="none" stroke-width="2"><path d="M20,17 Q150,0 280,17"/><path d="M20,17 Q150,34 280,17"/><circle cx="150" cy="17" r="6" fill="#222"/></g></svg>',
        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:12px; width:48px;" viewBox="0 0 100 24"><path d="M10,18 Q50,4 90,18" fill="none" stroke="#333" stroke-width="2.5"/><circle cx="50" cy="11" r="3.5" fill="#333"/></svg>'
      }
    ];

    let currentSetId = 1;
    let currentTab = 'all'; 
    let currentViewMode = 'single'; // 'single', 'all_main', 'all_sub', 'all_elem'

    function initApp() {
      const select = document.getElementById('theme-select');
      const listContainer = document.getElementById('sets-list-container');
      
      select.innerHTML = '';
      listContainer.innerHTML = '';

      TORAH_SETS.forEach(set => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = set.id;
        opt.textContent = 'סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name;
        select.appendChild(opt);

        const card = document.createElement('div');
        card.className = 'set-card' + (set.id === 1 ? ' active' : '');
        card.id = 'card-set-' + set.id;
        card.onclick = function() { selectTheme(set.id); };
        card.innerHTML = 
          '<div class="set-card-title">סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name + '</div>' +
          '<div class="set-card-desc">' + set.origin + '</div>';
        listContainer.appendChild(card);
      });

      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function selectTheme(id) {
      currentSetId = parseInt(id, 10);
      document.getElementById('theme-select').value = currentSetId;
      
      document.querySelectorAll('.set-card').forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('active'); });
      const activeCard = document.getElementById('card-set-' + currentSetId);
      if (activeCard) activeCard.classList.add('active');

      currentViewMode = 'single';
      document.getElementById('preview-tabs-bar').style.display = 'flex';
      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function handleSelectChange(val) {
      selectTheme(val);
    }

    function handleTabClick(tabName) {
      currentTab = tabName;
      document.querySelectorAll('.preview-tab').forEach(function(t) { t.classList.remove('active'); });
      const activeTabEl = document.getElementById('tab-' + tabName);
      if (activeTabEl) activeTabEl.classList.add('active');
      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function switchViewMode(mode) {
      currentViewMode = mode;
      document.getElementById('preview-tabs-bar').style.display = (mode === 'single' ? 'flex' : 'none');
      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function getMeta() {
      return {
        title: document.getElementById('meta-title').value || "שם הספר",
        subtitle: document.getElementById('meta-subtitle').value || "",
        desc: document.getElementById('meta-desc').value || "",
        sec: document.getElementById('meta-sec').value || "",
        author: document.getElementById('meta-author').value || "",
        year: document.getElementById('meta-year').value || ""
      };
    }

    function generateMainShaarHTML(set) {
      const meta = getMeta();
      return '' +
        '<div class="a4-page">' +
          set.renderMainSVG() +
          '<div class="shaar-main-content">' +
            '<div>' +
              '<div class="bsd-header">בס"ד</div>' +
              '<div class="sefer-label">ספר</div>' +
              '<div class="book-title-main">' + meta.title + '</div>' +
              '<div class="book-subtitle">' + meta.subtitle + '</div>' +
              '<div style="margin: 10px 0;">' + set.dividerSVG + '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="book-description">' + meta.desc + '</div>' +
            '<div class="book-sections-summary">' + meta.sec + '</div>' +
            '<div class="author-block">' +
              'חובר ונערך בס"ד ע"י<br>' +
              '<span class="author-name">' + meta.author + '</span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="publication-footer">' + meta.year + '</div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }

    function generateSubShaarHTML(set) {
      return '' +
        '<div class="a4-page sub-shaar-page">' +
          set.renderSubSVG() +
          '<div class="sub-shaar-content-area">' +
            '<div class="sefer-label">חלק</div>' +
            '<div class="book-title-main" style="font-size: 34pt;">חלק ראשון</div>' +
            '<div style="margin: 12px auto;">' + set.dividerSVG + '</div>' +
            '<div class="book-subtitle" style="font-size: 19pt;">ענייני תפילה ומועדים</div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }

    function generateElementsHTML(set) {
      return '' +
        '<div class="component-preview-card">' +
          '<div class="card-label">' +
            '<span>1. עיטור סיום פרק (Section Divider) - סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name + '</span>' +
            '<span style="color:#64748b; font-weight:normal;">רוחב מלא מעל גריד 2 טורים</span>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<div style="padding: 15px 0; text-align: center;">' +
            set.dividerSVG +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="component-preview-card">' +
          '<div class="card-label">' +
            '<span>2. עיטור מרכזי בכותרת העליונה (Header Center Gutter) - סט ' + set.id + '</span>' +
            '<span style="color:#64748b; font-weight:normal;">ממוקם מעל רווח הביניים בכותרת הרצה</span>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<div style="padding: 15px 0; display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; gap: 20px; font-weight: bold; font-size: 11pt;">' +
            '<span>חלק ראשון - ענייני תפילה</span>' +
            '<span>' + set.headerSVG + '</span>' +
            '<span>סימן א\' - דיני שחרית</span>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }

    function renderCurrentView() {
      const container = document.getElementById('display-container');
      container.innerHTML = '';

      if (currentViewMode === 'all_main') {
        TORAH_SETS.forEach(function(set) {
          const block = document.createElement('div');
          block.style.display = 'flex';
          block.style.flexDirection = 'column';
          block.style.alignItems = 'center';
          block.style.marginBottom = '40px';
          block.innerHTML = 
            '<div style="background:#0f172a; color:#facc15; padding:8px 24px; border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:15px; font-weight:bold; font-size:12pt; border:1px solid #b8860b;">' +
              'סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name + ' (' + set.origin + ')' +
            '</div>' +
            generateMainShaarHTML(set);
          container.appendChild(block);
        });
        return;
      }

      if (currentViewMode === 'all_sub') {
        TORAH_SETS.forEach(function(set) {
          const block = document.createElement('div');
          block.style.display = 'flex';
          block.style.flexDirection = 'column';
          block.style.alignItems = 'center';
          block.style.marginBottom = '40px';
          block.innerHTML = 
            '<div style="background:#0f172a; color:#facc15; padding:8px 24px; border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:15px; font-weight:bold; font-size:12pt; border:1px solid #b8860b;">' +
              'שער משנה - סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name +
            '</div>' +
            generateSubShaarHTML(set);
          container.appendChild(block);
        });
        return;
      }

      if (currentViewMode === 'all_elem') {
        TORAH_SETS.forEach(function(set) {
          const block = document.createElement('div');
          block.style.display = 'flex';
          block.style.flexDirection = 'column';
          block.style.alignItems = 'center';
          block.style.marginBottom = '25px';
          block.innerHTML = generateElementsHTML(set);
          container.appendChild(block);
        });
        return;
      }

      // Single Set View Mode
      const activeSet = TORAH_SETS.find(function(s) { return s.id === currentSetId; }) || TORAH_SETS[0];

      if (currentTab === 'all' || currentTab === 'main') {
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', generateMainShaarHTML(activeSet));
      }
      if (currentTab === 'all' || currentTab === 'sub') {
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', generateSubShaarHTML(activeSet));
      }
      if (currentTab === 'all' || currentTab === 'elem') {
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', generateElementsHTML(activeSet));
      }
    }

    window.onload = initApp;
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

### 🤖 תגובה 19: הנה גרסה מקיפה, עשירה ומשודרגת מהיסוד של קובץ התצוגה והעימוד ל-11 הסטים. ### מה…

הנה גרסה מקיפה, עשירה ומשודרגת מהיסוד של קובץ התצוגה והעימוד ל-11 הסטים. 

### מה שודרג בגרסה זו:
1. **סימטריה מתמטית מושלמת (100% Pixel-Symmetric)**: כל האלמנטים בכל 11 השערים מחושבים בציר מרכזי מדויק ($X = 397$) תוך שימוש במנגנון שיקוף וקטורי מובנה (`transform="translate(794,0) scale(-1,1)"`), מה שמבטיח שאין חריגה, סטייה או אי-התאמה של מילימטר בין צד ימין לצד שמאל.
2. **הפרדה מוחלטת ומניעת התנגשות שכבות (No Overlapping Elements)**: שטח הטקסט והטיפוגרפיה תָּחוּם בתוך אזור פנימי סטרילי ומוגן (Safe Zone), כך שאף עמוד, קשת, כתר או כרכוב אינם פולשים לטקסט או עולים זה על גבי זה.
3. **שערי משנה ייעודיים ועשירים לכל סט (11 עיצובים שונים לחלוטין)**: כל שער משנה יונק את השפה הארכיטקטונית המדויקת של השער הראשי שלו (לדוגמה: עמוד ספירלי וחצי גמלון שבור בסט 1; קרטוש תחרה וילנאית מלא בסט 2; קשת בארוק משוננת עם שושנת פינה בסט 3; כתר תורה וענפי אקנתוס בסט 4; רשת גיאומטרית וסרט בסט 5; גוויל ספר תורה פתוח בסט 6; שער היכל קלאסי בסט 7; פורטל כבד עם מדרגות תלת-ממד בסט 8; שטיח ערבסקה ולוחות ש"ס בסט 9; עמוד מסכתות וילנא ומזבח אש בסט 10; ואכסדרת ארמון בפרספקטיבה עם רצפת שיש בסט 11).
4. **עיטורי סיום פרק וכותרות עליונות ייחודיים לכל סט**: גזורים ישירות ממוטיב הסט (מזבח וילנא, רימוני ספר תורה, צדף בארוק, כתר ואקנתוס, פלמטה רומית, אבן ראשה וקמרונות וכו').

---

### קובץ ה-HTML המלא להרצה (`torah_11_themes_showcase.html`)

שמור את הקוד שלהלן כקובץ HTML ופתח אותו ישירות בדפדפן (Chrome / Edge / Firefox):

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="he" dir="rtl">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>מערכת 11 סטי שערים ועיטורים תורניים מפוארים לדפי A4</title>
<style>
  @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Bellefair&family=David+Libre:wght@400;700&family=Frank+Ruhl+Libre:wght@400;700;900&family=Noto+Serif+Hebrew:wght@400;700;900&family=Assistant:wght@400;600;700&display=swap');

  :root {
    --fn-sh-title: 'Bellefair', 'Frank Ruhl Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-title: 44pt;
    --fn-sh-sub: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-sub: 20pt;
    --fn-sh-desc: 'Frank Ruhl Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-desc: 11pt;
    --fn-sh-sec: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-sec: 11pt;
    --fn-sh-author: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-author: 14pt;
    --fn-sh-year: 'David Libre', serif;
    --sz-sh-year: 11.5pt;
  }

  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

  body {
    background-color: #0f172a;
    color: #0f172a;
    font-family: 'David Libre', 'Frank Ruhl Libre', serif;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    height: 100vh;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  /* Header Bar */
  #top-nav {
    background: #090d16;
    color: #f8fafc;
    padding: 10px 20px;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    border-bottom: 2px solid #b8860b;
    box-shadow: 0 2px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
    z-index: 1000;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .nav-title {
    font-size: 14pt;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #facc15;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
  }
  .nav-controls {
    display: flex;
    gap: 8px;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
  .set-picker {
    background: #1e293b;
    color: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #b8860b;
    padding: 6px 12px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    font-family: 'David Libre', serif;
    cursor: pointer;
    outline: none;
  }
  .btn-action {
    background: #b8860b;
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    padding: 6px 13px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    font-weight: 700;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 9.5pt;
    transition: 0.2s;
  }
  .btn-action:hover {
    background: #d4af37;
  }

  /* Live Metadata Bar */
  #metadata-bar {
    background: #f1f5f9;
    border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
    padding: 8px 16px;
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 12px;
    align-items: center;
    font-size: 9pt;
    font-family: 'Assistant', sans-serif;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .meta-field {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 5px;
  }
  .meta-field label {
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #334155;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .meta-field input {
    padding: 3px 6px;
    border: 1px solid #94a3b8;
    border-radius: 3px;
    font-size: 9pt;
    font-family: 'David Libre', serif;
  }

  /* Main Workspace */
  #main-workspace {
    display: flex;
    flex: 1;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  /* Sidebar */
  #sets-sidebar {
    width: 320px;
    background: #ffffff;
    border-left: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
    overflow-y: auto;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    padding: 12px;
    box-shadow: -2px 0 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .sidebar-section-title {
    font-size: 11pt;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #0f172a;
    border-bottom: 2px solid #b8860b;
    padding-bottom: 4px;
    margin-bottom: 10px;
  }
  .set-card {
    border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 10px;
    margin-bottom: 8px;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    background: #f8fafc;
  }
  .set-card:hover {
    border-color: #b8860b;
    background: #fffbeb;
  }
  .set-card.active {
    border-color: #b8860b;
    background: #fef08a;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(184, 134, 11, 0.4);
  }
  .set-card-title {
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    color: #0f172a;
  }
  .set-card-desc {
    font-size: 8.5pt;
    color: #64748b;
    margin-top: 3px;
    line-height: 1.3;
  }

  /* Preview Pane */
  #preview-pane {
    flex: 1;
    overflow-y: auto;
    padding: 25px 20px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 20px;
    background-color: #1e293b;
  }

  /* Tabs Bar */
  .preview-tabs {
    display: flex;
    gap: 6px;
    margin-bottom: -10px;
    z-index: 10;
  }
  .preview-tab {
    padding: 7px 16px;
    background: #0f172a;
    color: #94a3b8;
    border-radius: 6px 6px 0 0;
    font-weight: 700;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 10pt;
    border: 1px solid #334155;
    border-bottom: none;
    transition: 0.15s;
  }
  .preview-tab:hover {
    color: #fff;
  }
  .preview-tab.active {
    background: #ffffff;
    color: #0f172a;
    border-color: #ffffff;
  }

  /* A4 Page Representation */
  .a4-page {
    width: 210mm;
    height: 297mm;
    min-height: 297mm;
    max-height: 297mm;
    background: #ffffff;
    box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    padding: 0;
    margin-bottom: 25px;
  }

  /* Main Shaar Content Layer */
  .shaar-main-content {
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    position: relative;
    z-index: 20;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 24mm 16mm 20mm 16mm;
  }
  .bsd-header { font-size: 11pt; font-weight: 700; color: #333; }
  .sefer-label { font-size: 16pt; letter-spacing: 5px; font-weight: 700; margin-top: 4px; }
  .book-title-main { font-family: var(--fn-sh-title); font-size: var(--sz-sh-title); line-height: 1.08; margin: 8px 0 4px 0; font-weight: 900; }
  .book-subtitle { font-family: var(--fn-sh-sub); font-size: var(--sz-sh-sub); font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.25; }
  .book-description { font-family: var(--fn-sh-desc); font-size: var(--sz-sh-desc); line-height: 1.45; max-width: 68%; margin: 8px auto; }
  .book-sections-summary { font-family: var(--fn-sh-sec); font-size: var(--sz-sh-sec); line-height: 1.4; border-top: 1px solid #bbb; border-bottom: 1px solid #bbb; padding: 6px 12px; margin: 8px 0; font-weight: 700; width: 68%; }
  .author-block { font-family: var(--fn-sh-author); font-size: calc(var(--sz-sh-author) * 0.88); line-height: 1.3; }
  .author-name { font-size: var(--sz-sh-author); font-weight: 700; }
  .publication-footer { font-family: var(--fn-sh-year); font-size: var(--sz-sh-year); font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 1px; }

  /* Sub Shaar Content Layer */
  .sub-shaar-page {
    position: relative;
    padding: 0;
  }
  .sub-shaar-content-area {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0; bottom: 0; left: 0; width: 55%;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
    align-items: center;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 25mm 14mm;
    z-index: 20;
  }

  /* Component Showcase Cards */
  .component-preview-card {
    background: #ffffff;
    width: 210mm;
    padding: 20px 25px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    margin-bottom: 15px;
  }
  .card-label {
    font-size: 10.5pt;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #1e293b;
    margin-bottom: 12px;
    border-bottom: 1px dashed #cbd5e1;
    padding-bottom: 5px;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
  }

  .svg-bg-full {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%;
    pointer-events: none;
    z-index: 5;
  }

  @media print {
    body { background: none; overflow: visible; }
    #top-nav, #metadata-bar, #sets-sidebar, .preview-tabs { display: none !important; }
    #preview-pane { padding: 0; background: none; }
    .a4-page { box-shadow: none; margin: 0; page-break-after: always; break-after: page; }
  }
</style>
</head>
<body>

  <!-- Top Navigation Bar -->
  <div id="top-nav">
    <div class="nav-title">
      <span>👑</span>
      <span>11 סטי שערים ועיטורים תורניים מהודרים (A4)</span>
    </div>
    <div class="nav-controls">
      <select id="theme-select" class="set-picker" onchange="handleSelectChange(this.value)">
        <!-- Populated dynamically -->
      </select>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('single')">📄 סט נבחר</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('all_main')">📚 כל 11 השערים הראשיים</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('all_sub')">📑 כל שערי המשנה</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="switchViewMode('all_elem')">✨ כל העיטורים</button>
      <button class="btn-action" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ הדפסה / PDF</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Live Metadata Bar -->
  <div id="metadata-bar">
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>שם הספר:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-title" value="ספר מלכי המלוכה" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>כותרת משנה:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-subtitle" value="חידושים וביאורים עמוקים בסוגיות הש&quot;ס" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>נושא מפורט:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-desc" value="בירור שיטות הראשונים והאחרונים ובירורי הלכות למעשה בעיון נמרץ ובסברא ישרה" oninput="renderCurrentView()" style="width:250px;">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>פרטי מהדורא:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-sec" value="מהדורא שניה ומורחבת בסייעתא דשמיא" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>שם המחבר:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-author" value="ידידיה בר אבא הלוי" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
    <div class="meta-field">
      <label>מקום ושנה:</label>
      <input type="text" id="meta-year" value="ירושלים עיה&quot;ק תובב&quot;א — שנת ה'תשפ&quot;ו לפ&quot;ק" oninput="renderCurrentView()">
    </div>
  </div>

  <div id="main-workspace">
    <!-- Sidebar -->
    <div id="sets-sidebar">
      <div class="sidebar-section-title">11 ערכות הנושא (Sets)</div>
      <div id="sets-list-container">
        <!-- Populated dynamically -->
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Preview Workspace -->
    <div id="preview-pane">
      <div class="preview-tabs" id="preview-tabs-bar">
        <div class="preview-tab active" id="tab-all" onclick="handleTabClick('all')">כל 4 הרכיבים בסט זה</div>
        <div class="preview-tab" id="tab-main" onclick="handleTabClick('main')">שער ראשי (A4)</div>
        <div class="preview-tab" id="tab-sub" onclick="handleTabClick('sub')">שער משנה (A4)</div>
        <div class="preview-tab" id="tab-elem" onclick="handleTabClick('elem')">עיטורי סיום וכותרת</div>
      </div>

      <div id="display-container" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; width:100%;">
        <!-- Rendered output -->
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    /* Database of all 11 Authentic Torah Vector Sets */
    const TORAH_SETS = [
      {
        id: 1,
        name: "עמודי ספירלה וגמלון שבור",
        origin: "בהשראת תמונה 0 (אגרתא דחדוותא)",
        desc: "עמודים קורינתיים עטופים בסרט ספירלי מתלפף בתלת-מימד, גמלון שבור עליון עם קשת פנימית וכרכוב מדורג.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<defs>' +
              '<linearGradient id="grad-p-1" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="0">' +
                '<stop offset="0%" stop-color="#94a3b8"/><stop offset="30%" stop-color="#ffffff"/><stop offset="70%" stop-color="#e2e8f0"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#64748b"/>' +
              '</linearGradient>' +
              '<linearGradient id="grad-r-1" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">' +
                '<stop offset="0%" stop-color="#475569"/><stop offset="50%" stop-color="#0f172a"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#1e293b"/>' +
              '</linearGradient>' +
            '</defs>' +
            '<rect x="30" y="30" width="734" height="1063" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="36" y="36" width="722" height="1051" fill="none" stroke="#64748b" stroke-width="0.8" stroke-dasharray="6,3"/>' +
            '<g id="half-ped-1">' +
              '<path d="M 45 160 L 290 35 L 365 110 C 365 110 335 135 335 160 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
              '<path d="M 55 155 L 285 45 L 345 105" fill="none" stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<use href="#half-ped-1" transform="translate(794, 0) scale(-1, 1)"/>' +
            '<path d="M 335 160 C 335 90, 459 90, 459 160" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2.5"/>' +
            '<circle cx="397" cy="115" r="16" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
            '<circle cx="397" cy="115" r="5" fill="#111"/>' +
            '<rect x="45" y="160" width="704" height="36" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="52" y="166" width="690" height="24" fill="none" stroke="#64748b" stroke-width="1"/>' +
            '<g id="spiral-col-1">' +
              '<rect x="45" y="970" width="85" height="60" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
              '<rect x="55" y="980" width="65" height="40" fill="none" stroke="#64748b" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<circle cx="87" cy="1000" r="8" fill="none" stroke="#333" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<rect x="60" y="240" width="55" height="730" fill="url(#grad-p-1)" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
              '<line x1="72" y1="240" x2="72" y2="970" stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<line x1="87" y1="240" x2="87" y2="970" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
              '<line x1="102" y1="240" x2="102" y2="970" stroke="#64748b" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<path d="M 50 320 Q 87 360 125 330 L 125 380 Q 87 410 50 370 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<path d="M 50 470 Q 87 510 125 480 L 125 530 Q 87 560 50 520 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<path d="M 50 620 Q 87 660 125 630 L 125 680 Q 87 710 50 670 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<path d="M 50 770 Q 87 810 125 780 L 125 830 Q 87 860 50 820 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<path d="M 50 900 Q 87 940 125 910 L 125 955 Q 87 985 50 945 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1"/>' +
              '<path d="M 45 196 L 130 196 L 115 240 L 60 240 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<use href="#spiral-col-1" transform="translate(794, 0) scale(-1, 1)" />' +
            '<path d="M 130 270 C 130 210, 664 210, 664 270 L 664 970 L 130 970 Z" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        renderSubSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="30" y="30" width="734" height="1063" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="436" y="30" width="328" height="1063" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="none"/>' +
            '<path d="M 30 160 L 240 50 L 330 120 L 436 120 L 436 160 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="410" y="230" width="50" height="740" fill="url(#grad-p-1)" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
            '<path d="M 400 310 Q 435 345 470 320 L 470 355 Q 435 380 400 345 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111"/>' +
            '<path d="M 400 510 Q 435 545 470 520 L 470 555 Q 435 580 400 545 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111"/>' +
            '<path d="M 400 710 Q 435 745 470 720 L 470 755 Q 435 780 400 745 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111"/>' +
            '<path d="M 400 890 Q 435 925 470 900 L 470 935 Q 435 960 400 925 Z" fill="url(#grad-r-1)" stroke="#111"/>' +
            '<rect x="395" y="970" width="80" height="60" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 45 260 C 45 190, 410 190, 410 260 L 410 970 L 45 970 Z" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        dividerSVG: '<svg style="width:220px; height:26px; margin:auto;" viewBox="0 0 400 40"><g stroke="#222" fill="none" stroke-width="2"><path d="M10,20 L160,20"/><path d="M240,20 L390,20"/><circle cx="200" cy="20" r="12" fill="#fff" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2.5"/><circle cx="200" cy="20" r="5" fill="#222"/><path d="M175,10 Q200,20 175,30" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M225,10 Q200,20 225,30" stroke-width="2"/></g></svg>',
        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:11px; width:48px;" viewBox="0 0 100 24"><path d="M5,12 L40,12 M60,12 L95,12" stroke="#444" stroke-width="2"/><polygon points="50,4 58,12 50,20 42,12" fill="#444"/></svg>'
      },

      {
        id: 2,
        name: "פסי תחרה וילנאית רחבים",
        origin: "בהשראת תמונה 1 (ספר ברכת הלוי)",
        desc: "שולי תחרה וילנאית עשירים ורחבים בשני צדי הדף עם תבנית סלסולים צפופה ומסגרת מרכזית עדינה.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +

        desc: "שער ש\"ס וילנא המסורתי והאותנטי – עמודי סדרי המשנה, מזבח אש תמיד בבסיס וקשת ענפים עליונה.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {

              '<line x1="35" y1="720" x2="120" y2="720" stroke="#333" stroke-width="1"/>' +

            '<use href="#vilna-side-col-10" transform="translate(794, 0) scale(-1, 1)"/>' +
            '<g transform="translate(317, 920)">' +
              '<rect x="20" y="40" width="120" height="50" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
              '<circle cx="80" cy="20" r="16" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
              '<path d="M 75 22 Q 80 8 85 22 Z" fill="#111"/>' +
            '</g>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        renderSubSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="20" y="20" width="754" height="1083" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="436" y="20" width="338" height="1083" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="none"/>' +
            '<g transform="translate(350, 200)">' +
              '<rect x="0" y="0" width="85" height="700" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="100" x2="85" y2="100" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="200" x2="85" y2="200" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="300" x2="85" y2="300" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="400" x2="85" y2="400" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="500" x2="85" y2="500" stroke="#333"/>' +
              '<line x1="0" y1="600" x2="85" y2="600" stroke="#333"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<path d="M 40 260 C 40 120, 350 120, 350 260" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        dividerSVG: '<svg style="width:200px; height:24px; margin:auto;" viewBox="0 0 240 30"><g fill="#222"><polygon points="120,4 128,15 120,26 112,15"/><polygon points="90,7 96,15 90,23 84,15"/><polygon points="150,7 156,15 150,23 144,15"/><line x1="10" y1="15" x2="75" y2="15" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/><line x1="165" y1="15" x2="230" y2="15" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/></g></svg>',
        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:12px; width:45px;" viewBox="0 0 90 24"><polygon points="45,4 52,12 45,20 38,12" fill="#222"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="30" y2="12" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/><line x1="60" y1="12" x2="85" y2="12" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"/></svg>'
      },

      {
        id: 11,
        name: "אכסדרת ארמון בפרספקטיבה עם עומק ורצפת שיש",
        origin: "בהשראת תמונה 10 (שולחן ערוך המבואר - מתיבתא)",
        desc: "שער פרספקטיבה תלת-מימדי מהודר עם רצפת שיש מדורגת, עמודי שיש כפולים, וילונות קשורים וקשת מקומרת.",
        renderMainSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<g stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1" fill="#f8fafc">' +
              '<polygon points="200,820 594,820 744,980 50,980"/>' +
              '<line x1="397" y1="820" x2="397" y2="980"/>' +
              '<line x1="330" y1="820" x2="220" y2="980"/><line x1="464" y1="820" x2="574" y2="980"/>' +
              '<line x1="265" y1="820" x2="70" y2="980"/><line x1="529" y1="820" x2="724" y2="980"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<path d="M 200 320 C 200 150, 594 150, 594 320 L 594 820 L 200 820 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 230 340 C 230 190, 564 190, 564 340 L 564 820 L 230 820 Z" fill="none" stroke="#64748b" stroke-width="1.2"/>' +
            '<rect x="40" y="40" width="714" height="80" fill="#f1f5f9" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2.5"/>' +
            '<rect x="70" y="160" width="85" height="700" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="639" y="160" width="85" height="700" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="40" y="980" width="714" height="70" fill="#e2e8f0" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2.5"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        renderSubSVG: function() {
          return '<svg class="svg-bg-full" viewBox="0 0 794 1123" preserveAspectRatio="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
            '<rect x="40" y="40" width="714" height="1043" fill="none" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<rect x="436" y="40" width="318" height="1043" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="none"/>' +
            '<g stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1" fill="#f8fafc">' +
              '<polygon points="120,820 436,820 436,980 50,980"/>' +
              '<line x1="280" y1="820" x2="280" y2="980"/><line x1="200" y1="820" x2="140" y2="980"/>' +
            '</g>' +
            '<rect x="375" y="160" width="60" height="700" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="2"/>' +
            '<path d="M 50 320 C 50 160, 375 160, 375 320 L 375 820 L 50 820 Z" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#111" stroke-width="1.8"/>' +
          '</svg>';
        },
        dividerSVG: '<svg style="width:230px; height:26px; margin:auto;" viewBox="0 0 300 35"><g stroke="#222" fill="none" stroke-width="2"><path d="M20,17 Q150,0 280,17"/><path d="M20,17 Q150,34 280,17"/><circle cx="150" cy="17" r="6" fill="#222"/></g></svg>',
        headerSVG: '<svg style="height:12px; width:48px;" viewBox="0 0 100 24"><path d="M10,18 Q50,4 90,18" fill="none" stroke="#333" stroke-width="2.5"/><circle cx="50" cy="11" r="3.5" fill="#333"/></svg>'
      }
    ];

    let currentSetId = 1;
    let currentTab = 'all'; 
    let currentViewMode = 'single'; // 'single', 'all_main', 'all_sub', 'all_elem'

    function initApp() {
      const select = document.getElementById('theme-select');
      const listContainer = document.getElementById('sets-list-container');
      
      select.innerHTML = '';
      listContainer.innerHTML = '';

      TORAH_SETS.forEach(set => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = set.id;
        opt.textContent = 'סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name;
        select.appendChild(opt);

        const card = document.createElement('div');
        card.className = 'set-card' + (set.id === 1 ? ' active' : '');
        card.id = 'card-set-' + set.id;
        card.onclick = function() { selectTheme(set.id); };
        card.innerHTML = 
          '<div class="set-card-title">סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name + '</div>' +
          '<div class="set-card-desc">' + set.origin + '</div>';
        listContainer.appendChild(card);
      });

      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function selectTheme(id) {
      currentSetId = parseInt(id, 10);
      document.getElementById('theme-select').value = currentSetId;
      
      document.querySelectorAll('.set-card').forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('active'); });
      const activeCard = document.getElementById('card-set-' + currentSetId);
      if (activeCard) activeCard.classList.add('active');

      currentViewMode = 'single';
      document.getElementById('preview-tabs-bar').style.display = 'flex';
      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function handleSelectChange(val) {
      selectTheme(val);
    }

    function handleTabClick(tabName) {
      currentTab = tabName;
      document.querySelectorAll('.preview-tab').forEach(function(t) { t.classList.remove('active'); });
      const activeTabEl = document.getElementById('tab-' + tabName);
      if (activeTabEl) activeTabEl.classList.add('active');
      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function switchViewMode(mode) {
      currentViewMode = mode;
      document.getElementById('preview-tabs-bar').style.display = (mode === 'single' ? 'flex' : 'none');
      renderCurrentView();
    }

    function getMeta() {
      return {
        title: document.getElementById('meta-title').value || "שם הספר",
        subtitle: document.getElementById('meta-subtitle').value || "",
        desc: document.getElementById('meta-desc').value || "",
        sec: document.getElementById('meta-sec').value || "",
        author: document.getElementById('meta-author').value || "",
        year: document.getElementById('meta-year').value || ""
      };
    }

    function generateMainShaarHTML(set) {
      const meta = getMeta();
      return '' +
        '<div class="a4-page">' +
          set.renderMainSVG() +
          '<div class="shaar-main-content">' +
            '<div>' +
              '<div class="bsd-header">בס"ד</div>' +
              '<div class="sefer-label">ספר</div>' +
              '<div class="book-title-main">' + meta.title + '</div>' +
              '<div class="book-subtitle">' + meta.subtitle + '</div>' +
              '<div style="margin: 10px 0;">' + set.dividerSVG + '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="book-description">' + meta.desc + '</div>' +
            '<div class="book-sections-summary">' + meta.sec + '</div>' +
            '<div class="author-block">' +
              'חובר ונערך בס"ד ע"י<br>' +
              '<span class="author-name">' + meta.author + '</span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="publication-footer">' + meta.year + '</div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }

    function generateSubShaarHTML(set) {
      return '' +
        '<div class="a4-page sub-shaar-page">' +
          set.renderSubSVG() +
          '<div class="sub-shaar-content-area">' +
            '<div class="sefer-label">חלק</div>' +
            '<div class="book-title-main" style="font-size: 34pt;">חלק ראשון</div>' +
            '<div style="margin: 12px auto;">' + set.dividerSVG + '</div>' +
            '<div class="book-subtitle" style="font-size: 19pt;">ענייני תפילה ומועדים</div>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }

    function generateElementsHTML(set) {
      return '' +
        '<div class="component-preview-card">' +
          '<div class="card-label">' +
            '<span>1. עיטור סיום פרק (Section Divider) - סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name + '</span>' +
            '<span style="color:#64748b; font-weight:normal;">רוחב מלא מעל גריד 2 טורים</span>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<div style="padding: 15px 0; text-align: center;">' +
            set.dividerSVG +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="component-preview-card">' +
          '<div class="card-label">' +
            '<span>2. עיטור מרכזי בכותרת העליונה (Header Center Gutter) - סט ' + set.id + '</span>' +
            '<span style="color:#64748b; font-weight:normal;">ממוקם מעל רווח הביניים בכותרת הרצה</span>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<div style="padding: 15px 0; display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; gap: 20px; font-weight: bold; font-size: 11pt;">' +
            '<span>חלק ראשון - ענייני תפילה</span>' +
            '<span>' + set.headerSVG + '</span>' +
            '<span>סימן א\' - דיני שחרית</span>' +
          '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }

    function renderCurrentView() {
      const container = document.getElementById('display-container');
      container.innerHTML = '';

      if (currentViewMode === 'all_main') {
        TORAH_SETS.forEach(function(set) {
          const block = document.createElement('div');
          block.style.display = 'flex';
          block.style.flexDirection = 'column';
          block.style.alignItems = 'center';
          block.style.marginBottom = '40px';
          block.innerHTML = 
            '<div style="background:#0f172a; color:#facc15; padding:8px 24px; border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:15px; font-weight:bold; font-size:12pt; border:1px solid #b8860b;">' +
              'סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name + ' (' + set.origin + ')' +
            '</div>' +
            generateMainShaarHTML(set);
          container.appendChild(block);
        });
        return;
      }

      if (currentViewMode === 'all_sub') {
        TORAH_SETS.forEach(function(set) {
          const block = document.createElement('div');
          block.style.display = 'flex';
          block.style.flexDirection = 'column';
          block.style.alignItems = 'center';
          block.style.marginBottom = '40px';
          block.innerHTML = 
            '<div style="background:#0f172a; color:#facc15; padding:8px 24px; border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:15px; font-weight:bold; font-size:12pt; border:1px solid #b8860b;">' +
              'שער משנה - סט ' + set.id + ': ' + set.name +
            '</div>' +
            generateSubShaarHTML(set);
          container.appendChild(block);
        });
        return;
      }

      if (currentViewMode === 'all_elem') {
        TORAH_SETS.forEach(function(set) {
          const block = document.createElement('div');
          block.style.display = 'flex';
          block.style.flexDirection = 'column';
          block.style.alignItems = 'center';
          block.style.marginBottom = '25px';
          block.innerHTML = generateElementsHTML(set);
          container.appendChild(block);
        });
        return;
      }

      // Single Set View Mode
      const activeSet = TORAH_SETS.find(function(s) { return s.id === currentSetId; }) || TORAH_SETS[0];

      if (currentTab === 'all' || currentTab === 'main') {
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', generateMainShaarHTML(activeSet));
      }
      if (currentTab === 'all' || currentTab === 'sub') {
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', generateSubShaarHTML(activeSet));
      }
      if (currentTab === 'all' || currentTab === 'elem') {
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', generateElementsHTML(activeSet));
      }
    }

    window.onload = initApp;
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## פנייה 6: יפה אך לא מושלם.... נסה שוב!! ונראה לי ששערי המשנה צרכים להיות כמו השערים…

יפה אך לא מושלם....
נסה שוב!!
ונראה לי ששערי המשנה צרכים להיות כמו השערים הראשיים, מכווצים לחצי השמאלי של הדף.
